Second transcription's note: A return with a Schedule C is treated as answering Yes, whatever the interview answer.

Ruling: representation; project retained. Step 5 question 2 (self-employed) is answered from the return: a Schedule C on the return is self-employment and, within scope, the only kind (partnership, farm and church employee income are refused). The second asks the filer as well and treats a Schedule C as Yes; equal for every return in scope.

Second transcription's note: Several STOP or referral points are not computed and are refused instead. Step 1, question 3 (Form 2555) is refused through sa.excluded_income, which also covers Form 4563 and Puerto Rico. Step 1, question 4 (nonresident alien) is refused through scope.other_income. Step 2, question 4 (Schedule E, personal property rental, Form 8814, passive activity) is refused through scope.schedule1, scope.other_income and scope.other_line16. Step 6 (the IRS figures the credit) is not modeled.

Ruling: refusal representation; project retained. As ruled before: the project's expansion differs by the scope gates it carries as refusals on Steps 1 and 2; the Step 1 to 5 tests appear on both sides. Stage 14 adds Worksheet B: both use line 4b for a return with a Schedule C, and where the second stops on line 4b zero or less the project enters zero on Worksheet B line 6 (eic.wsb_6), so the credit is zero on both.

### eic.check_27c — The instructions say to check the box on line 27c (a STOP that says so, or Worksheet A lin
Status: **DISAGREE**

Second transcription's note: Only the first STOP reached in the flowchart counts. STOPs that do not say to check line 27c (Step 1, question 1; Step 2; Step 3, question 5; Step 4, questions 1, 2 and 6; Step 5, question 3) end the flow without setting the box.

Ruling: representation and scope; project retained; equivalence not proved. As ruled before: the project's expansion differs by the scope gates it carries as refusals, and it counts each STOP that says to check line 27c only where the flow chart reaches it, plus Worksheet A line 2 zero; ten Stage 9 fixtures assert it. Since BUG-0057 it also counts the Worksheet B line 5 STOP (eic.wsb_line5_stop; stage14.eic.wsb_line5_stop_27c), and the line 4b STOP reaches the box through Worksheet A line 2 zero (stage14.eic.wsb_stop_27c).

Ruling: representation; project retained. Both choose the EIC Table column by the number of qualifying children with valid SSNs; the project adds the guard for an earned income below the table's first row ($1), which has no printed row (ambiguity register amb.2025.eic_below_table).

### 3 — Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b
Status: **AGREE**

### 4 — Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?
Status: **AGREE**

### 5 — Look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit, using the column for you
Status: **DISAGREE**

```

Ruling: equivalent representation; project retained. As ruled before under line 6: the same choice (line 2 when line 4 is Yes or line 3 is below the line 5 amount; the smaller of lines 5 and 2 otherwise) written as two guards instead of nested choices. The line is now keyed by its id because Worksheet B also prints a line 6 (BUG-0054).

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| eic.credit | eic.clergy, eic.h_church, s8812.combat_pay, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, eic.h_church |
| (Step 5, 1) | eic.clergy, eic.h_church | eic.clergy, eic.h_church |
| (Step 5, 2) | scope.schedule1 | scope.schedule1 |
| (Step 5, 3) | eic.clergy, eic.h_church, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, eic.h_church |
| (Step 5, 4) | s8812.combat_pay | - |
| (Step 5, 5) | eic.clergy, eic.h_church, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, eic.h_church |
| 4b | eic.clergy, eic.h_church, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, eic.h_church |
| 6 (eic.wsb_6) | eic.clergy, eic.h_church, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, eic.h_church |
| 1 | eic.clergy, eic.h_church, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, eic.h_church |
| 2 | eic.clergy, eic.h_church, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, eic.h_church |
| 4 | eic.clergy, eic.h_church, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, eic.h_church |
| 6 (eic.line6) | eic.clergy, eic.h_church, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy, eic.h_church |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 1)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    refuses: [eic.clergy, eic.h_church]
    note: "Step 5, question 1 sends clergy and church employees filing Schedule SE to special rules that change line 1. This tool refuses those cases."

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 2)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "Schedule 1, line 8s is outside the tool's Schedule 1 scope, so this line is zero. A filer with such payments is refused by the Schedule 1 screen."

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 3)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: "eic.line(Step 5, 1)", take: "eic.line(Step 5, 2)"}

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 4)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay]

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 5)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: ["eic.line(Step 5, 3)", "eic.line(Step 5, 4)"]}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_1e_2c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, lines 1e and 2c, for you and your spouse if filing jointly"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.eic_se}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, line 3. Enter the amount from Schedule C, line 1, that you are filing as a statutory employee"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    note: "The tool screens statutory employee Schedules C (sc.statutory), so line 3 is zero here."

  - id: eic.line4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.h_wsb_1e_2c, eic.h_wsb_3, "eic.line(Step 5, 5)"]}
    note: "Line 4a ('your earned income from Step 5') is Step 5, line 5."

  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: eic.line4b, take: {const: 0}}
    note: "If line 4b is zero or less, the worksheet stops before line 6. Here line 6 is zero in that case, and eic.credit and eic.check_27c reflect the stop."

  - id: eic.h_se_answer
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: input, source: "EIC Step 5, question 2"}

  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 2: self-employed (Worksheet B)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [eic.h_se_answer, s1.has_sc]}
    note: "A return with a Schedule C is treated as answering Yes, whatever the interview answer."

  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}, then: eic.wsb_6}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}, then: "eic.line(Step 5, 5)"}
    note: "Worksheet B, lines 7 to 11 repeat Worksheet A, lines 2 to 6, with Worksheet B line 6 in place of line 1. This line is Worksheet A line 1 or Worksheet B line 6, and lines 2 to 6 below serve as Worksheet A lines 2-6 or Worksheet B lines 7-11."

  - id: eic.h_children
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn_child, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_mfj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing status is married filing jointly"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}

  - id: eic.h_lookup1_0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, no qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
    note: "Uses the married filing jointly column when status is mfj, and otherwise the single/head of household/qualifying surviving spouse column (which the star footnote also assigns to married filing separately). Amounts below the first row ($1) and amounts at or above the column's '*' cutoff give 0."

  - id: eic.h_lookup1_1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, one qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_lookup1_2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, two qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_lookup1_3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, three or more qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 0}]}, then: eic.h_lookup1_0}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 1}]}, then: eic.h_lookup1_1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 2}]}, then: eic.h_lookup1_2}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_children, {const: 3}]}, then: eic.h_lookup1_3}

  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}

  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}

  - id: eic.h_lookup3_0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, no qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_lookup3_1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, one qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_lookup3_2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, two qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_lookup3_3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, three or more qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 0}]}, then: eic.h_lookup3_0}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 1}]}, then: eic.h_lookup3_1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 2}]}, then: eic.h_lookup3_2}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_children, {const: 3}]}, then: eic.h_lookup3_3}
    note: "The worksheet leaves line 5 blank when line 4 is Yes or line 3 is under the threshold. Here the lookup is always computed, and line 6 uses it only when the worksheet says to."

  - id: eic.h_ws0_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,620"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.other}

  - id: eic.h_ws0_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$17,730 (married filing jointly)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}

  - id: eic.h_ws1_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$23,350"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.other}

  - id: eic.h_ws1_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$30,470 (married filing jointly)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}

  - id: eic.h_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold: no qualifying children $10,620 ($17,730 if married filing jointly); 1 or more $23,350 ($30,470 if married filing jointly)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 0}]}, eic.h_mfj]}, then: eic.h_ws0_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, not: eic.h_mfj}]}, then: eic.h_ws0_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_children, {const: 1}]}, eic.h_mfj]}, then: eic.h_ws1_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_children, {const: 1}]}, {op: test, not: eic.h_mfj}]}, then: eic.h_ws1_other}

  - id: eic.h_below
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Is the amount on line 3 less than the threshold for your number of qualifying children?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_threshold]}

  - id: eic.h_use_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Enter the amount from line 2 on line 6 (line 4 is Yes, or line 5 answered Yes)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [eic.line4, eic.h_below]}

  - id: eic.h_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Look at the amounts on lines 5 and 2. Then, enter the smaller amount on line 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}

  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6 (eic.line6)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_use_line2, {const: true}]}, then: eic.line2}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_use_line2, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_smaller}

  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}

  - id: eic.h_lim0_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$19,104"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.other}

  - id: eic.h_lim0_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$26,214 (married filing jointly)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.joint}

  - id: eic.h_lim1_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$50,434"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.other}

  - id: eic.h_lim1_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$57,554 (married filing jointly)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.joint}

  - id: eic.h_lim2_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$57,310"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.other}

  - id: eic.h_lim2_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$64,430 (married filing jointly)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.joint}

  - id: eic.h_lim3_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$61,555"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.other}

  - id: eic.h_lim3_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$68,675 (married filing jointly)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.joint}

  - id: eic.h_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Income limit for your number of children who have valid SSNs and filing status (Step 1, question 1; Step 5, question 3; Worksheet B, line 5)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 0}]}, eic.h_mfj]}, then: eic.h_lim0_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, not: eic.h_mfj}]}, then: eic.h_lim0_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 1}]}, eic.h_mfj]}, then: eic.h_lim1_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 1}]}, {op: test, not: eic.h_mfj}]}, then: eic.h_lim1_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 2}]}, eic.h_mfj]}, then: eic.h_lim2_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 2}]}, {op: test, not: eic.h_mfj}]}, then: eic.h_lim2_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_children, {const: 3}]}, eic.h_mfj]}, then: eic.h_lim3_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_children, {const: 3}]}, {op: test, not: eic.h_mfj}]}, then: eic.h_lim3_other}

  - id: eic.h_agi_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 1: is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than the limit?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.h_7a_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040, line 7a (if line 7a is a loss, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [f1040.line7a, eic.h_zero]}

  - id: eic.h_investment
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 1: Investment income. Add Form 1040 or 1040-SR, lines 2a, 2b, 3b and 7a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line2a, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, eic.h_7a_pos]}

  - id: eic.h_invest_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$11,950"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.investment_limit}

  - id: eic.h_invest_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 2: investment income is not more than $11,950"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: [eic.h_investment, eic.h_invest_limit]}
    note: "If investment income is over $11,950, question 3 asks about Form 4797. Form 4797 is refused by sd.other_forms, so the only answer computed is No, which means STOP. The Step 2, question 4 situations (Schedule E, rental of personal property, Form 8814, passive activity) are refused on eic.credit."

  - id: eic.h_pre
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 and 2 passed"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [eic.h_agi_ok, household.all_work_ssn, eic.h_invest_ok]}
    note: "household.all_work_ssn stands for Step 1, question 2 (an SSN valid for employment, and so valid for the EIC). Step 1, questions 3 and 4 are handled by refusals on eic.credit."

  - id: eic.h_qc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of children who meet the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_has_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 1: Do you have at least one child who meets the conditions to be your qualifying child for the purpose of claiming the EIC?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1}]}

  - id: eic.h_married_sep
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you a married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - eic.married
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}]}

  - id: eic.h_sep_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, questions 4 and 5 passed: you and your spouse did not have the same principal residence for the last 6 months of 2025, or you are legally separated and lived apart at the end of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, not: eic.same_home}, eic.legally_separated]}

  - id: eic.h_step3_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 passed (filers with a qualifying child)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - eic.h_mfj
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, not: eic.qc_of_another}
            - {op: test, any: [{op: test, not: eic.h_married_sep}, eic.h_sep_ok]}

  - id: eic.h_age_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of people on the return born after December 31, 1960, and before January 2, 2001"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_before_2001, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_after_1960, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_age_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 2: Were you, or your spouse if filing a joint return, at least age 25 but under age 65 at the end of 2025?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_age_count, {const: 1}]}
    note: "The instructions refer to Pub. 596 when a spouse or the filer died in 2025, and that rule is not given here. This line uses the birth-date test as written."

  - id: eic.h_step4_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 passed (filers without a qualifying child)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, not: eic.h_married_sep}
        - eic.h_age_ok
        - eic.us_home
        - op: test
          any:
            - eic.h_mfj
            - {op: test, all: [{op: test, not: eic.qc_of_another}, {op: test, not: sa.dependent}]}
    note: "Step 4, question 6 (can you be claimed as a dependent) is read from sa.dependent. That input also covers a spouse and dual-status aliens, and it is asked only when not filing jointly."

  - id: eic.h_reach5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 through 4 passed; go to Step 5"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - eic.h_pre
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, all: [eic.h_has_qc, eic.h_step3_pass]}
            - {op: test, all: [{op: test, not: eic.h_has_qc}, eic.h_step4_pass]}

  - id: eic.h_step5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 3 / Worksheet B, line 5: earned income less than the limit (and Worksheet B, line 4b more than zero)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, not: eic.self_employed}, {op: test, gt: [eic.line4b, {const: 0}]}]}

  - id: eic.h_eligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 to 5 passed"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [eic.h_reach5, eic.h_step5]}

  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit for Form 1040, line 27a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: true}]}, then: eic.line6}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_zero}
    refuses: [sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1, scope.other_line16]
    note: "Several STOP or referral points are not computed and are refused instead. Step 1, question 3 (Form 2555) is refused through sa.excluded_income, which also covers Form 4563 and Puerto Rico. Step 1, question 4 (nonresident alien) is refused through scope.other_income. Step 2, question 4 (Schedule E, personal property rental, Form 8814, passive activity) is refused through scope.schedule1, scope.other_income and scope.other_line16. Step 6 (the IRS figures the credit) is not modeled."

  - id: eic.h_stop_ssn
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "STOP at Step 1, question 2 (check the box on line 27c)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [eic.h_agi_ok, {op: test, not: household.all_work_ssn}]}

  - id: eic.h_stop_s3q6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "STOP at Step 3, question 6 (check the box on line 27c)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - eic.h_pre
        - eic.h_has_qc
        - {op: test, not: eic.h_mfj}
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, not: eic.h_married_sep}, eic.h_sep_ok]}
        - eic.qc_of_another

  - id: eic.h_stop_s4q3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "STOP at Step 4, question 3 (check the box on line 27c)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - eic.h_pre
        - {op: test, not: eic.h_has_qc}
        - {op: test, not: eic.h_married_sep}
        - eic.h_age_ok
        - {op: test, not: eic.us_home}

  - id: eic.h_stop_s4q5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "STOP at Step 4, question 5 (check the box on line 27c)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - eic.h_pre
        - {op: test, not: eic.h_has_qc}
        - {op: test, not: eic.h_married_sep}
        - eic.h_age_ok
        - eic.us_home
        - {op: test, not: eic.h_mfj}
        - eic.qc_of_another

  - id: eic.h_stop_wsa
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet A, line 2 is zero (check the box on line 27c)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - eic.h_reach5
        - {op: test, not: eic.self_employed}
        - {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.line2, {const: 0}]}

  - id: eic.h_stop_wsb
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B STOP at line 4b, line 5 or line 7 (check the box on line 27c)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - eic.h_reach5
        - eic.self_employed
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, le: [eic.line4b, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, ge: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.line2, {const: 0}]}

  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on line 27c (instructed by a STOP in the line 27a instructions)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any: [eic.h_stop_ssn, eic.h_stop_s3q6, eic.h_stop_s4q3, eic.h_stop_s4q5, eic.h_stop_wsa, eic.h_stop_wsb]
    note: "Only the first STOP reached in the flowchart counts. STOPs that do not say to check line 27c (Step 1, question 1; Step 2; Step 3, question 5; Step 4, questions 1, 2 and 6; Step 5, question 3) end the flow without setting the box."

  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - eic.h_pre
        - eic.h_has_qc
        - {op: test, not: eic.h_mfj}
        - eic.h_married_sep
        - eic.h_sep_ok
        - {op: test, not: eic.qc_of_another}

  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you a minister, member of a religious order who has not taken a vow of poverty, or a Christian Science practitioner filing Schedule SE, with an amount on line 2 of that schedule also reported on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: input, source: "Form 1040, line 27b; EIC Step 5, question 1 (Clergy)"}

  - id: eic.h_church
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you filing Schedule SE because you had church employee income of $108.28 or more?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: input, source: "EIC Step 5, question 1 (Church employees)"}
```
```
